genid, {"run_full_eval": run_next_eval})\n\ndef select_next_parent_container(\n    docker_client,\n    domains,\n    generate_output_dir,\n    archive,\n    root_dir="./",\n    root_commit="HEAD",\n    max_attempts=10,\n):\n    # Setup logger\n    logger = setup_logger(os.path.join(generate_output_dir, "select_next_parent.log"))\n\n    # Get the latest node that can select parent\n    latest_node = get_latest_can_select_parent(archive, generate_output_dir)\n    safe_log(f"select_next_parent_container: latest_node={latest_node}")\n    prev_patch_files = get_patch_files(generate_output_dir, "/tmp/"\n\n    try:\n        # Apply all lineage diffs\n        commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, prev_patch_files, verbose=False)\n\n        # Copy generate_output_dir to container\n source_path=generate_output_dir,\n            dest_path=container_generate_output_dir,\n            verbose=False,\n        )\n\n        # Get next parent in container\n        command = [\n            "timeout",\n            "3600",  # 1h timeout\n            "python",\n            "-m",\n            "utils.run_select_next_parent",\n            "--domains",\n            *domains,\n log_container_output(exec_result, verbose=True)\n\n        # Get next parent outputs\n        container_output_strings = exec_result.output.decode().strip().split("\\n")\n        next_parent_genid = container_output_strings[-1]\n        next_parent_genid = int(next_parent_genid) if not is_starting_node(next_parent_genid) else next_parent_genid\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        safe_log(f"Error in select_next_parent_container: {e}")\n        update_node_metadata(generate_output_dir, latest_node, {"can_select_next_parent": False})\n        next_parent_genid = None\n\n    # Even on errors or KeyboardInterrupt\n    finally:\n        # Reset to the root commit\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(\n            cmd=["git", "reset", "--hard", root_commit], no parent is selected\n    if next_parent_genid is None:\n        if max_attempts > 0:\n            next_parent_genid = select_next_parent_container(\n                docker_client,\n                domains,\n                generate_output_dir,\n                archive,\n                root_dir,\n                root_commit,\n                max_attempts=max_attempts - 1,\n            )\n        else:\n            # In case of infinite recursive, but it should not happen\n            raise Exception("Max attempts reached in select_next_parent_container")\n\n    return next_parent_genid\n\ndef get_ensemble_scores_container(\n    docker_client,\n    domain,\n    generate_output_dir,\n    gen_output_dir,\n    root_dir="./",\n    root_commit="HEAD",\n    prev_patch_files=[],\n    num_samples=-1,\n    max_workers=5,\n    subsets=[],\n):\n    # Setup logger\n    logger = setup_logger(os.path.join(gen_output_dir, "ensemble.log"))\n\n    # Create and start the Docker container\n    image_name = f"{REPO_NAME}"\n    run_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")\n    container_name = f"{REPO_NAME}-ens-container-{run_id}"\n    container = build_container(docker_client, root_dir, image_name, container_name)\n    container.start()\n    container_output_folder = "/tmp/"\n\n    try:\n        # Apply all lineage diffs\n        commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, prev_patch_files)\n\n        # Copy generate_output_dir to container\n        container_generate_output_dir = os.path.join(\n            container_output_folder, generate_output_dir.split(os.sep)[-1]\n        )\n        copy_to_container(\n            container,\n            source_path=generate_output_dir,\n            dest_path=container_generate_output_dir,\n        )\n\n        # Get ensemble scores in container\n        scores = []\n        for subset in subsets:\n            command = [\n                "timeout",\n                "10800",  # 3h timeout\n                "python",\n                "-m",\n                "utils.run_ensemble",\n                "--domain",\n                domain,\n                "--generate_output_dir",\n                container_generate_output_dir,\n                "--num_samples",\n                str(num_samples),\n                "--max_workers",\n                str(max_workers),\n                "--subset",\n                subset,\n            ]\n            exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}")\n            log_container_output(exec_result)\n\n            # Get ensemble outputs\n            container_output_strings = exec_result.output.decode().strip().split("\\n")\n            score = float(container_output_strings[-3])\n            scores.append(score)\n# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.\n\nfrom agent.base_agent import AgentSystem\nfrom agent.llm_withtools import chat_with_agent\n\nclass MetaAgent(AgentSystem):\n    def forward(self, repo_path, eval_path, iterations_left=None):\n        """\n        A meta agent that recursively self-improves.\n\n        Args:\n            repo_path (str): The path to the repository.\n            eval_path (str): The path to previously generated agents and their evaluation results.\n            iterations_left (int, optional): The number of remaining iterations in which the meta agent will be invoked in future. Defaults to None.\n        """\n        instruction = f"Modify any part of the codebase at `{repo_path}`."\n        # [experiment adaptation, see DECISIONS.md] gpt-6-luna otherwise asks for clarification and never edits.\n        instruction += (\n            f"\\n\\nThe repository\'s README.md describes the goal. "\n            "Use the tools to inspect the code and make your changes; a response without a tool call ends the session, so only reply without one when you are done."\n        )\n\n Incomplete, explicitly skipped, or invalid generations\\n    are not treated as scored candidates.\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n    completed = defaultdict(list)\\n    incomplete = []\\n    if not os.path.isdir(eval_path):\\n        return f\\"No prior evaluation directory found at {eval_path!r}; inspect available evidence before making performance claims.\\"\\n\\n    for dirname in sorted(os.listdir(eval_path)):\\n        if dirname != \\"gen_initial\\" and not (dirname.startswith(\\"gen_\\") and dirname[4:].isdigit()):\\n            continue\\n        gen_dir = os.path.join(eval_path, dirname)\\n        if not os.path.isdir(gen_dir):\\n            continue\\n        metadata = {}\\n        metadata_path = os.path.join(gen_dir, \\"metadata.json\\")\\n        try:\\n            with open(metadata_path) as f:\\n                metadata = json.load(f)\\n            if not isinstance(metadata, dict):\\n                metadata = {}\\n        except (OSError, ValueError):\\n            pass\\n\\n        report_paths = []\\n        for child in sorted(os.listdir(gen_dir)):\\n            report_path = os.path.join(gen_dir, child, \\"report.json\\")\\n            if child.endswith(\\"_eval\\") and os.path.isfile(report_path):\\n                report_paths.append(report_path)\\n        if not report_paths:\\n            if metadata.get(\\"run_eval\\") is False:\\n                incomplete.append(f\\"{dirname}: evaluation skipped\\")\\n            elif metadata.get(\\"valid_parent\\") is False:\\n                incomplete.append(f\\"{dirname}: invalid generation\\")\\n            continue\\n\\n        for report_path in report_paths:\\n            domain = os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(report_path))[:-5]\\n            label = f\\"{dirname}/{domain}\\"\\n            if metadata.get(\\"valid_parent\\") is False:\\n                incomplete.append(f\\"{label}: invalid generation (report excluded)\\")\\n                continue\\n            if metadata.get(\\"run_eval\\") is False:\\n                incomplete.append(f\\"{label}: evaluation marked skipped (report excluded)\\")\\n                continue\\n            try:\\n                with open(report_path) as f:\\n                    report = json.load(f)\\n            except (OSError, ValueError):\\n                incomplete.append(f\\"{label}: unreadable report\\")\\n                continue\\n            if not isinstance(report, dict):\\n                incomplete.append(f\\"{label}: malformed report\\")\\n                continue\\n\\n            scores = {}\\n            raw_scores = report.get(\\"per_task_score\\")\\n            if isinstance(raw_scores, dict):\\n                for task_id, value in raw_scores.items():\\n                    if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool):\\n                        value = float(value)\\n                        if math.isfinite(value) and 0 <= value <= 1:\\n                            scores[str(task_id)] = value\\n            score = report.get(\\"overall_accuracy\\")\\n            if not isinstance(score, (int, float)) or isinstance(score, bool):\\n incomplete.append(f\\"{label}: missing or invalid accuracy\\")\\n                continue\\n            score = float(score)\\n\\n            if scores:\\n                cohort = (\\"task_ids\\", tuple(sorted(scores)))\\n                cohort_label = f\\"{len(scores)} exact task IDs\\"\\n            else:\\n                sample_count = report.get(\\"total_test_inputs\\", report.get(\\"total\\"))\\n                if isinstance(sample_count, bool) or not isinstance(sample_count, int) or sample_count < 0:\\n                    sample_count = None\\n                cohort = (\\"count\\", sample_count)\\n                cohort_label = \\"unknown-size cohort\\" if sample_count is None else f\\"{sample_count} tasks (IDs unavailable)\\"\\n            completed[(domain, cohort)].append({\\n                \\"generation\\": \\"initial\\" if dirname == \\"gen_initial\\" else dirname[4:],\\n                \\"score\\": score,\\n                \\"parent\\": metadata.get(\\"parent_genid\\"),\\n                \\"task_scores\\": scores,\\n                \\"cohort_label\\": cohort_label,\\n            })\\n\\n    lines = [\\n        \\"Prior evaluation evidence (compare scores only within the same domain and exact task cohort when available; matching counts without IDs are weaker evidence):\\"\\n    ]\\n    if not completed:\\n        lines.append(\\"- No completed, valid reports found. Inspect available reports and metadata before making performance claims.\\")\\n    for (domain, cohort), records in sorted(completed.items(), key=lambda item: (item[0][0], repr(item[0][1]))):\\n        records.sort(key=lambda r: (-r[\\"score\\"], str(r[\\"generation\\"])))\\n        lines.append(f\\"- {domain}, {records[0][\'cohort_label\']}: top candidates and baseline:\\")\\n        selected = records[:max_candidates]\\n        baseline = next((r for r in records if r[\\"generation\\"] == \\"initial\\"), None)\\n        if baseline is not None and baseline not in selected:\\n            selected.append(baseline)\\n        for record in selected:\\n            parent = \\"\\" if record[\\"parent\\"] is None else f\\", parent {record[\'parent\']}\\"\\n            failures = [task for task, value in record[\\"task_scores\\"].items() if value < 1.0]\\n            fail_text = f\\"; failing task IDs: {\', \'.join(sorted(failures)[:10])}\\" if failures else \\"\\"\\n            lines.append(f\\"  gen_{record[\'generation\']}={record[\'score\']:.4f}{parent}{fail_text}\\")\\n\\n        # Repeated failures across runs identify useful places to investigate, not\\n        # proven causes; keep the denominator explicit and only combine exact cohorts.\\n        task_records = [record for record in records if record[\\"task_scores\\"]]\\n        if task_records:\\n            failed = Counter()\\n            for record in task_records:\\n                failed.update(task for task, value in record[\\"task_scores\\"].items() if value < 1.0)\\n            recurrent = [(task, count) for task, count in failed.most_common(6) if count > 1]\\n            if recurrent:\\n                lines.append(\\"  Recurrent failures (failed runs / reports with task scores): \\" + \\", \\".join(\\n                    f\\"{task} {count}/{len(task_records)}\\" for task, count in recurrent\\n                ))\\n    if incomplete:\\n        examples = \\"; \\".join(incomplete[:8])\\n        suffix = \\" ...\\" if len(incomplete) > 8 else \\"\\"\\n        lines.append(f\\"- Excluded/incomplete runs: {len(incomplete)} ({examples}{suffix}).\\")\\n    return \\"\\\\n\\".join(lines)\\n\\n\\nclass MetaAgent(AgentSystem):\\n    def forward(self, repo_path, eval_path, iterations_left=None):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Recursively improve the agent using repository and evaluation evidence.\\"\\"\\"\\n        instruction = (\\n            f\\"Modify any part of the codebase at `{repo_path}`.\\\\n\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"The repository\'s README.md describes the goal. Previously generated agents and their evaluation results are at `{eval_path}`. \\"\\n            \\"Work autonomously: nobody will answer questions, so do not ask for clarification.\\\\n\\\\n\\"\\n            \\"Use the evaluation summary below as a starting point, then inspect the relevant reports, metadata, and patches yourself. \\"\\n            \\"Choose changes based on comparable evaluation evidence, not generation number or recency. Recurrent failing task IDs are hypotheses to investigate, not proof of a shared cause. \\"\\n            \\"Favor focused changes, preserve behavior supported by the strongest comparable result, and distinguish completed evaluations from skipped, partial, invalid, or incomparable runs. \\"\\n            \\"Do not claim a change improved performance without a comparable evaluation.\\\\n\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"{_evaluation_summary(eval_path)}\\\\n\\\\n\\"\\n            \\"Use the tools to inspect the code and make your changes; a response without a tool call ends the session, so only reply without one when you are done.\\"\\n        )\\n        return chat_with_agent(\\n            instruction,\\n            model=self.model,\\n            msg_history=[],\\n            logging=self.log,\\n            tools_available=\\"all\\",\\n        )\\nPY\\npython -m py_compile meta_agent.py && python - <<\'PY\'\\nimport json, os, tempfile\\nfrom meta_agent import
